We further derive stronger dimension-dependent lower bounds for tail index $p>4/3$, showing that a nontrivial dependence on the dimension is unavoidable in the low-dimensional regime. Based on these estimators, we propose a quantum normalized stochastic gradient descent method ($\texttt{QNSGD}$), which finds an $ε$-stationary point using $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}\big(\sqrt d\,ε^{-\frac{5p-4}{2p-2}}\big)$ queries to the quantum stochastic gradient oracle. For a convex objective function, we propose a quantum projected stochastic gradient descent method ($\texttt{QPSGD}$), which computes a solution with $ε$-optimal solution using $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}\big(\sqrt d\,ε^{-\frac{3p-2}{2p-2}}+ε^{-2}\big)$ queries in expectation. These sharper bounds improve upon the classical lower bounds $Ω\big(ε^{-\frac{3p-2}{p-1}}\big)$ for nonconvex problems and $Ω\big(ε^{-\frac{p}{p-1}}\big)$ for convex problems in the low-dimensional regimes $d\lesssimε^{-\frac{p}{p-1}}$ and $d\lesssimε^{-\frac{2-p}{p-1}}$, respectively.

</details>


### [126] [Anti-Backdoor Coreset Selection via Cumulative Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25502)
*Qi Zhao,Christian Wressnegger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出反后门核心集选择方法，通过累积熵跟踪学习动态，从 poisoned 数据中筛选良性样本训练无后门模型，在保持自然性能的同时有效防御后门攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练时防御方法需要从受污染的训练数据中隔离出良性子集来训练无后门模型，但缺乏系统的选择策略，且常牺牲自然准确率或无法应对某些攻击。

Method: 将防御策略形式化为核心集选择问题，利用累积熵作为选择标准跟踪样本学习动态，并结合逐轮遗忘所选样本以增强良性与有毒样本的可分离性。

Result: 该方法能一致地缓解后门攻击，且对自然性能的影响可忽略不计，优于此前牺牲自然准确率或无法应对某些攻击的防御方法。

Conclusion: 反后门核心集选择是一种有效的训练时防御方法，通过聚焦良性样本的学习动态，实现了防御效果与自然性能的良好平衡。

Abstract: Recent training-time defenses against neural backdoors isolate a benign subset from poisoned training data, to learn a backdoor-free model from it. In this paper, we formulate this defense strategy as a coreset selection problem, giving rise to so-called "Anti-Backdoor Coreset Selection." Since poisonous samples have (a) lower prediction uncertainty and are (b) less frequent than benign samples, coreset selection naturally focuses more on samples associated with benign functionality than the backdoor functionality. We use the Cumulative Entropy as selection criterion to further facilitate this effect. The metric tracks the learning dynamics of training samples and allowing us to select benign samples with high informativeness for the coreset. Additionally, we unlearn the chosen samples in each epoch to facilitate the separability between benign and poisonous samples. Together, this yields an exceptionally effective training-time defense that constructs a benign coreset to train a backdoor-free model. Unlike prior defenses that compromise natural accuracy and fail against certain attacks, our method mitigates backdooring attacks consistently with a negligible impact on natural performance.

</details>


### [127] [AMPBench-MT: A Homology-Controlled Benchmark for Antimicrobial Peptide Potency, Spectrum, and Safety Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25518)
*Ziheng Zhou,Huiyu Luo,Xiaohu Zhu,Nan Wang,Xuebiao Qin,Chaoyan Zhang,Jun Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AMPBench-MT基准，用于联合评估AMP的二元识别、物种条件效价、溶血毒性、选择性等多维度指标，发现高二元识别性能不能可靠预测实际检测行为，呼吁AMP评估应转向端点感知的证据审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AMP基准仅关注二元识别或多标签注释等单一任务，缺乏一个在序列同源性控制下联合评估AMP识别、物种条件效价、抗菌谱、安全性代理终点及跨端点行为的标准化协议。

Method: 构建AMPBench-MT基准，标准化规范肽记录，组织为二元识别、物种条件pMIC回归、端点特异性效价和安全性读数三大任务，并进行161项端点特异性模型评估。

Result: 高二元识别性能不能可靠指示检测端点行为；蛋白质语言模型嵌入在pMIC误差上领先，图模型和经典回归器表现接近；稀缺负样本下PR指标具有误导性；低毒性、HC50溶血和选择性暴露出更小但更具检测意义的信号。

Conclusion: AMP评估应超越识别排行榜，转向端点感知的证据审计。

Abstract: Computational AMP discovery is often evaluated through AMP/non-AMP recognition, yet follow-up decisions depend on assay-derived evidence such as target-species potency, hemolysis, toxicity, and selectivity. Existing AMP and peptide benchmarks cover binary recognition, multilabel annotation, assay regression, or broader peptide-model comparison, but they do not jointly place AMP recognition, species-conditioned potency, spectrum, safety-facing proxy endpoints, and cross-endpoint behavior within one sequence-homology-controlled protocol. To address this problem, we introduce AMPBench-MT, a provenance-preserving benchmark that standardizes canonical peptide records and organizes them into binary recognition, species-conditioned pMIC regression, and endpoint-specific potency and safety-facing readouts. Across 161 endpoint-specific model evaluations, high binary performance does not reliably indicate assay-endpoint behavior. Frozen protein-language-model embeddings form the leading pMIC error cluster, while graph and classical regressors remain close. Spectrum labels further reveal that PR-oriented metrics can be misleading under scarce observed negatives, whereas low-toxicity, HC50 hemolysis, and selectivity expose smaller but more assay-facing signals. AMPBench-MT shows that AMP evaluation should move beyond recognition leaderboards toward endpoint-aware evidence auditing. Our proposed benchmark is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/ZihengZhou06/AMPBench-MT.

</details>


### [128] [Multi-Scale Structural Features for Continual, Comprehensible Visual Recognition in a Developmental Learning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25531)
*Zeki Doruk Erden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的多尺度视觉特征表示方法，将其集成到无梯度、发育式学习框架中，用于持续学习二维形状识别。该方法在类增量MNIST基准测试中显著提高了识别准确率，匹配或超越了使用重放和正则化的基线方法，同时无需存储历史数据，并保持了持续学习的关键特性——旧知识不被覆盖，学习表征具备可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习在持续学习、复用先验知识和提供可理解的内部结构方面存在困难。先前提出的无梯度发育式学习框架虽然具备持续学习能力，但其视觉特征表示表达能力有限，限制了识别准确率。

Method: 引入了一种新的多尺度视觉特征表示，编码边缘和轮廓特征及其空间关系；将其集成到网络精炼学习过程中；改进了学习动态和用于预测的读出机制。

Result: 新方法在准确率上显著优于先前的表示方法，匹配或超越了使用重放和正则化的基线方法；保留了持续学习行为，即早期学习的类别在引入新类别时不会被破坏性适应所覆盖；基线方法会在自己的周期内放弃刚学过的类别而后重新学习，但本方法不会。

Conclusion: 该方法以逐样本方式整合信息，同时可证明地保留了对之前输入的响应，实现了真正的持续学习，无需重放缓冲区或预定义任务边界。

Abstract: Contemporary machine learning struggles to learn continually, reuse prior knowledge, and expose a comprehensible internal structure. A recently proposed developmental, gradient-free learning framework addresses these limitations by learning a discrete, topological model of its inputs through local variation and selection, yielding an inherent continual-learning guarantee: new observations refine existing structure without overwriting past knowledge, and without replay buffers or predefined task boundaries. Its extension to visual inputs demonstrated this principle on shape recognition, but relied on a feature representation of limited expressivity that capped recognition accuracy. We introduce a new visual feature representation that encodes shape structure across multiple scales, capturing edge and contour features together with their spatial relations, and integrate it with the network-refinement learning process; we further improve the learning dynamics and the read-out used to predict from the learned model. The study targets two-dimensional shape, with class-incremental MNIST as a controlled, interpretable benchmark in which continual-learning behavior can be measured directly. Our approach substantially increases accuracy over the prior representation, matching or exceeding replay- and regularisation-based baselines at comparable storage while storing no past data, and preserves the framework's defining behavior: earlier-learned classes are retained as new ones are introduced, with no destructive adaptation, and the learned representations remain human-interpretable. What separates the methods is retention: the baselines surrender most of a just-trained class within its own cycle and relearn it afterwards, which ours does not. The significance lies in the manner of learning. The system integrates information one sample at a time while provably preserving its responses to...

</details>


### [129] [Mind the Missing Split: Resolving Feature Heterogeneity in Swarm Learning with Random Forests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25538)
*Mohammad Tajabadi,Dominik Heider*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在群体学习（Swarm Learning）中，针对随机森林模型因各站点特征部分重叠导致的推理时特征缺失问题，设计了多种确定性及概率性推理策略，实验证明其优于仅使用特征交集训练或本地模型的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际应用中，不同站点的数据集往往只有部分特征重叠（如测量协议、协变量不同），而传统水平群体学习假设各站点特征集相同。这导致随机森林在全局聚合后，本地推理时可能遇到不存在的分割特征，迫使组织丢弃特有特征，从而降低模型性能。

Method: 提出了多种推理时策略来处理缺失分割问题，包括确定性方法和概率性方法。这些策略不限制训练时仅使用特征交集，而是允许利用所有站点的完整特征集，在推理阶段通过不同机制处理局部缺失的特征分裂节点。

Result: 在9个数据集上进行了评估，结果表明所提出的方法在多种场景下均优于仅使用特征交集训练的基线模型以及各站点独立训练的本地模型。

Conclusion: 所提出的推理时策略有效解决了群体学习中随机森林面临的特征异质性挑战，为实际应用中部署去中心化协作学习提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: Swarm Learning is a decentralized collaborative learning mechanism that allows multiple organizations to train a shared model without central coordination or direct data sharing. In typical horizontal Swarm Learning, datasets across sites are usually assumed to share the same feature set. However, in real-world applications, sites often have partially overlapping features because measurements, protocols, and available covariates differ across sites. This feature heterogeneity creates a practical issue for machine learning algorithms such as Random Forests. Specifically, when decision trees are pooled into a global Random Forest, inference at a given site can become ill-defined if a traversal encounters a split on a feature that is not available locally, often forcing organizations to discard site-specific variables upfront. In this paper, we address feature heterogeneity in Swarm Learning with Random Forests under partially overlapping feature spaces. We propose several deterministic and probabilistic inference-time strategies that resolve such missing splits without restricting training to the intersection of features. We evaluate the methods on nine datasets and demonstrate that they outperform both the intersection baseline and locally trained models across a broad range of scenarios.

</details>


### [130] [OrthKD: Extracting Generalized Clinical Knowledge from Heterogeneous Teachers for Lightweight Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25545)
*Yi Xu,Cheng Chen,Mufan Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OrthKD选择性蒸馏框架，通过区分强CNN教师（完整监督）和弱ViT教师（仅特征蒸馏）并强制正交投影，在边缘设备上实现高准确率、鲁棒且校准良好的糖尿病视网膜病变筛查模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多教师知识蒸馏假设所有教师提供同等可信的监督信号，但在DR筛查场景中，弱Transformer教师（0.830 QWK）的logits会误导学生模型，需要一种选择性信任的蒸馏方法。

Method: 提出OrthKD框架：从强CNN教师进行完整logits+特征蒸馏，从弱ViT教师仅进行特征蒸馏，并强制两个教师对应的学生投影向量正交，以鼓励互补而非冗余的证据学习。

Result: MobileNetV3学生模型（5.4M参数）在EyePACS上达0.885 QWK，零样本迁移至Messidor-2从0.507提升至0.728 QWK，同时保持优异的转诊AUC和校准性能。

Conclusion: 选择性蒸馏异构教师能够使DR筛查模型在资源受限的边缘设备上实现实际部署，兼顾准确率、安全性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Deploying diabetic retinopathy (DR) screening models in primary care requires edge-efficient systems that remain accurate, safe, and reliable under domain shift. Multi-teacher knowledge distillation (KD) is a natural compression strategy, but existing approaches largely assume that all teachers provide equally trustworthy supervision. In our setting, this assumption fails: a strong CNN teacher (EfficientNet-B3, 0.876 QWK) and a weaker Transformer teacher (Swin-Base, 0.830 QWK) are complementary, yet the Transformer's logits can still mislead the student. We therefore propose OrthKD, a selective-trust distillation framework that transfers full supervision from the strong CNN, uses feature-only distillation from the weak ViT, and enforces orthogonality between teacher-specific student projections to encourage complementary rather than redundant evidence. This design preserves local lesion precision, injects global structural context, and improves robustness to distribution shift. On 132,049 retinal images, a 5.4M-parameter MobileNetV3 student reaches 0.885 QWK on EyePACS and improves zero-shot Messidor-2 performance from 0.507 to 0.728 QWK, while also achieving strong referral AUC and calibration. These results show that selectively distilling heterogeneous teachers can enable practical DR screening on resource-constrained devices.

</details>


### [131] [Physics-Informed Broad Learning System: An Efficient Backpropagation-Free Framework for Solving Partial Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25608)
*Pinki Khatun,M. Sajid,Abhinav Jha,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于物理信息的宽度学习系统(PI-BLS)，通过将物理约束嵌入线性输出层优化问题，用伪逆求解替代传统PINNs的非线性梯度训练，显著降低了计算成本并保持了物理约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PINNs依赖昂贵的梯度优化和深层架构，导致训练慢、计算成本高、可扩展性有限。

Method: 将控制微分算子及初边值约束直接嵌入线性输出层优化问题，通过伪逆求解确定性的最小二乘解，将整个学习过程简化为单阶段线性优化。

Result: 在代表性前向PDE基准测试中，PI-BLS以更少的训练时间和模型参数取得了具有竞争性甚至更优的性能。

Conclusion: PI-BLS提供了一种高效的物理信息学习范式，消除了迭代反向传播，同时保留了物理约束。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for solving partial differential equations (PDEs) by embedding governing physical laws into deep neural networks. However, their reliance on computationally expensive gradient-based optimization and deep architectures often results in slow training, high computational cost, and limited scalability. In this work, we propose a novel physics-informed broad learning system (PI-BLS), the first physics-informed learning framework based on broad RdNNs. The proposed formulation embeds the governing differential operator and the associated initial and boundary constraints directly into a linear output-layer optimization problem, thereby replacing nonlinear gradient-based training with a deterministic least-squares solution obtained via the pseudoinverse. Consequently, the entire learning process is reduced to a single linear optimization stage while preserving the underlying physical constraints. As a result, PI-BLS offers an efficient learning paradigm for a physics-informed learning framework for solving PDEs that eliminates iterative backpropagation while preserving the underlying physical constraints. Experimental results on representative forward PDE benchmarks demonstrate that PI-BLS achieves competitive and often superior performance with reduced training time and model parameters compared with conventional PINNs.

</details>


### [132] [Contrastive Representation Learning of Longitudinal Disease Trajectories on Temporal Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25609)
*Bastian Pfeifer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出对比表示学习框架，将疾病轨迹建模为时序图，通过对比图神经网络学习表征，实现患者聚类和潜在结构发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从纵向临床数据中理解疾病轨迹具有挑战性，因为存在复杂的时序动态和异质性患者群体。

Method: 将多变量疾病轨迹建模为时序图（节点为患者观测，边捕获时间连续性和结构相似性），利用结构感知随机游走引导对比学习生成保留时序上下文和轨迹拓扑的嵌入。

Result: 生成的表征能够实现具有相似疾病进程模式的患者鲁棒聚类，并揭示纵向数据中的潜在结构。

Conclusion: 该对比图神经网络框架能有效建模复杂疾病轨迹，发现患者亚群和潜在数据模式。

Abstract: Understanding disease trajectories from longitudinal clinical data remains challenging due to complex temporal dynamics and heterogeneous patient cohorts. Here, we present a contrastive representation learning framework that models multivariate disease trajectories as temporal graphs and learns representations using contrastive graph neural networks. Nodes represent patient observations over time, while edges capture temporal continuity and structural similarity between trajectories. Structure-aware random walks guide contrastive learning to generate embeddings that preserve temporal context and trajectory topology. The resulting representations enable robust clustering of patients with similar disease progression patterns and reveal latent structure in longitudinal data.

</details>


### [133] [MemSFT: Mitigating Alignment Tax with an External Parametric Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25614)
*Jiarui Wang,Xiang Shi,Jiaqi Cao,Rubin Wei,Xiquan Wang,Hao Sun,Jingzhi Wang,Zhiqi Yang,Qipeng Guo,Bowen Zhou,Zhouhan Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MemSFT通过即插即用的参数化记忆模块解耦领域专业化与骨干参数更新，在提升领域性能的同时几乎不损害通用能力，有效缓解了对齐税问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调大语言模型到专业领域会产生对齐税（alignment tax），即领域微调会导致灾难性遗忘，显著降低通用任务性能。

Method: 提出MemSFT，使用可学习的参数化记忆模块模仿非参数化检索器在领域数据上的行为，通过动态路由器在每步解码时融合记忆与骨干网络的输出分布，实现领域知识的按需调用。

Result: 在生物学、地球科学和法律三个领域，使用Qwen3-8B到Qwen3-235B-A22B模型评估，MemSFT一致提升领域性能，通用性能几乎无下降，而全参数微调出现严重的通用遗忘。

Conclusion: MemSFT提供了一种在参数层面解耦通用模型能力与领域特定知识的实用路径，使LLM获得新专业能力的同时不牺牲通用能力。

Abstract: Adapting Large Language Models (LLMs) to specialized domains often incurs an alignment tax, as fine-tuning on domain-specific tasks can cause catastrophic forgetting and substantially degrade performance on general tasks. We propose MemSFT, which mitigates the alignment tax by decoupling domain specialization from backbone parameter updates through a plug-and-play parametric memory. The memory is trained to imitate the behavior of a non-parametric retriever operating over domain data, thereby memorizing knowledge and patterns that would otherwise be accessed through retrieval. Once trained on a specific domain, the memory can be reused across LLMs of different sizes. During generation, a learned router dynamically fuses the output distributions of the memory and backbone at each decoding step, allowing domain expertise to be invoked selectively. Across biology, geoscience, and law, evaluations with models ranging from Qwen3-8B to Qwen3-235B-A22B show that MemSFT consistently improves domain performance with negligible degradation in general performance, whereas full SFT suffers severe forgetting on general tasks. Overall, our results demonstrate a practical path to decoupling general model capabilities from domain-specific knowledge at the parameter level, thereby equipping LLMs with new specialized capabilities without compromising their general capabilities.

</details>


### [134] [Tools Are Not Islands: Set-Level Tool Retrieval for LLM Agents via Query-Conditioned Hyperedge Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25718)
*Xinyi Hong,Pinjun Dong,Xinyang Yu,Binyan Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HYSET方法，将工具检索建模为基于超边预测的集合级检索，在ToolBench上优于现有方法，并支持零样本/少样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具检索方法要么独立评分每个工具，要么顺序构建工具集合，缺乏对候选工具集整体联合效用的评估。

Method: 将工具检索形式化为查询条件化的超边预测问题，在工具共调用超图上建模，通过基数特定的交互捕获工具间依赖关系，作为预选择模块无需修改下游智能体。

Result: 在ToolBench上，HYSET在工具检索性能和端到端任务成功率上均持续优于最先进基线，并支持零样本/少样本迁移到未见工具和领域。

Conclusion: HYSET通过集合级评估有效提升工具检索质量，且作为即插即用模块具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly rely on invoking external tools to complete real-world tasks. Tool retrieval, which selects a small task-relevant subset from a library of thousands of tools before the agent acts, has therefore become a critical component of LLM agent pipelines. However, existing retrievers either score each tool in isolation or assemble the tool set sequentially, so the joint utility of a candidate set is never evaluated as a whole. In this paper, we propose HYSET, short for HYperedge-based SEt-level Tool retrieval. Our contributions are threefold: (i) we formulate tool retrieval as query-conditioned hyperedge prediction on a tool co-invocation hypergraph, under which the tool set itself becomes the unit of scoring and most existing retrieval paradigms reduce to restricted instances; (ii) we capture size-dependent tool compatibility through cardinality-specific interactions; and (iii) we design HYSET as a pre-selection module requiring no modification to the downstream agent. Experiments on ToolBench demonstrate that HYSET consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in both tool retrieval performance and end-to-end task success. Beyond the in-domain setting, HYSET further supports zero-shot/few-shot transfer, generalizing to held-out tools/categories and unseen domains with minimal supervision.

</details>


### [135] [Using Data-Derived Priors to Guide CNN Architecture Design for NIR Chemometrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25636)
*Dário Passos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过对25个近红外回归任务的分析，发现光谱数据集的特征（如熵、内在秩、小波能量支撑分数等）可以为卷积神经网络（CNN）的设计提供经验先验，特别是卷积感受野的选择。基于这些关系构建的启发式规则在性能上与贝叶斯超参数优化相当，而联合预处理和CNN优化在多数任务中优于仅使用标准化光谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近红外化学计量学中，CNN设计常依赖通用架构规则，但不同光谱数据集在采样、平滑性、冗余度和样本量方面差异显著。作者希望验证这些光谱属性是否能为CNN设计提供经验先验，从而更高效地指导模型架构选择。

Method: 在25个NIR回归任务上计算了数据集大小、光谱长度与间距、熵、内在秩、自相关及小波尺度结构等描述符。设计了两种可解释1D-CNN架构（最小单卷积模型和扩展浅层模型），通过五折交叉验证的贝叶斯超参数优化（HPO）进行训练。从近最优试验中提取关系转换为暖启动启发式规则，并通过直接验证和留一数据集（LODO）验证评估。还测试了联合预处理与CNN优化的效果。

Result: 最显著的关系涉及卷积感受野：最小CNN中，优选核比例随光谱熵和内在秩增加而降低，随小波能量支撑分数增加而升高；学习率随训练集大小增加而降低。直接和LODO启发式规则与HPO竞争力相当（中位测试RMSE比分别为0.953和1.017）。扩展CNN结构展现出类似但迁移性较差的模式。联合预处理和CNN HPO在19/25个任务中优于仅标准化光谱的HPO。

Conclusion: 光谱描述符可为CNN设计提供实用的先验知识，在特定目标调优之前，能够将浅层NIR模型引导至合理的超参数区域，从而提升建模效率和性能。

Abstract: Convolutional neural networks (CNN) for near-infrared (NIR) chemometrics are often designed using generic architectural rules, although spectral datasets differ in sampling, smoothness, redundancy, and sample size. We tested whether these properties can provide empirical priors for CNN design. Across 25 NIR regression tasks, we computed descriptors of dataset size, spectral length and spacing, entropy, intrinsic rank, autocorrelation, and wavelet-scale structure. Two interpretable 1D-CNN scaffolds (a minimal single-convolution model and an extended shallow model with optional branching, dilation, etc) were optimized using five-fold cross-validated Bayesian hyperparameter optimization (HPO). Relationships extracted from near-optimal trials were converted into warm-start heuristics and evaluated directly and through leave-one-dataset-out (LODO) validation. The clearest relationships involved convolutional receptive fields. In the minimal CNN, the preferred kernel fraction decreased with spectral entropy and intrinsic rank, increased with the wavelet energy-support fraction, and the learning rate tended to decrease with training-set size. Direct and LODO heuristics were competitive with HPO, with median test-RMSE ratios of 0.953 and 1.017, respectively. The extended CNN showed similar but less transferable structure across branch usage, dilation, dropout, filter counts, and receptive-field choices. Ten stochastic refits showed seed sensitivity comparable to that of HPO-selected configurations. In a separate experiment, joint preprocessing and CNN HPO outperformed standardized-spectra HPO in 19 of 25 tasks, although gains were dataset-dependent. These results show that spectral descriptors can provide practical CNN design priors, guiding shallow NIR models toward plausible hyperparameter regions before target-specific tuning

</details>


### [136] [Contextual Deconvolution for Variance-Stable Demand Sensing: Kernel-Modulated Operators in Promotional Retail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25664)
*Mohammad Forouhesh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出上下文反卷积（CD）两阶段估计器，通过凸分解将促销冲击与结构基线分离，降低安全库存和订单波动，但在持有成本超过缺货成本约20%时方能降低总成本，主要贡献在于提高预测可靠性而非中心准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习需求预测虽优化统计精度，但剩余运营波动导致安全库存膨胀和牛鞭效应加剧，需在预测中分离促销驱动的瞬时冲击与稳定的结构性基线。

Method: 提出Contextual Deconvolution（CD）两阶段估计器：核调制带状算子实现促销冲击与结构基线的凸分解，层次化部分池化支持无需单SKU训练的目录级部署，算子数据驱动设计。

Result: 在M5（30,490 SKU）和Favorita（2,845项）上评估：CD最低的截面误差离散度，0.8%的SKU误预测率远低于基线（9.9-20.6%）；仅当持有成本超过缺货成本约20%时（95% CI [17%,25%]）降低总成本，否则仅提供运营稳定性和库存资本层。

Conclusion: CD的核心贡献在于预测可靠性（低离散度）而非中心趋势准确性；方差比和标准差安全库存应作为诊断指标而非优化目标；所学需求算子非正态但紧凑参数核可解释匹配运营性能。

Abstract: Machine learning demand forecasts optimize statistical accuracy yet leave excess operational volatility that inflates safety stock and amplifies the Bullwhip effect. We introduce \textbf{Contextual Deconvolution} (CD), a two-stage estimator that reframes demand sensing as a convex decomposition: a kernel-modulated banded operator separates transient promotion-driven shocks from a smooth structural baseline, and hierarchical partial pooling enables catalog-scale deployment without per-SKU training. The operator is data-derived, not imposed---it reduces to the identity wherever the promotional response is impulsive (most of M5, all of Favorita) and contributes only where genuine multi-day carryover exists, so the gains rest on the structural decomposition itself. Evaluating strictly out-of-sample on 30,490 M5 SKUs and 2,845 Favorita items, with calendar-aware baselines given CD's identical future calendar, we anchor the contribution on a full inventory-cost accounting: CD lowers safety stock, holding cost, and order variance but under-provisions event spikes, reducing total cost only when holding costs exceed $\sim$20\% of stockout costs (95\% CI $[17\%,25\%]$); otherwise it is an operational-stability and inventory-capital layer, not an expected-cost minimizer. Its accuracy contribution is reliability rather than central tendency: across eleven baselines, CD attains the lowest cross-sectional dispersion of per-SKU error and mis-forecasts by more than 200\% on 0.8\% of SKUs versus 9.9--20.6\% for every baseline, ranking first on both in all four M5 draws. Because the Variance Ratio and std-based safety stock are minimized by any sufficiently smooth forecast, we treat them as diagnostics, not objectives. A supporting analysis shows the learned demand operators are non-normal, yet CD's compact parametric kernel matches their operational performance interpretably.

</details>


### [137] [A Physics-Informed Neural Operator for Thermal Ranking of Low-Cost Wall Materials in Hot-Dry Climates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25668)
*Muhammad Akbar Khan,Fahim Raees,Ubaida Fatima*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段计算框架，用于评估五种低成本本土墙体材料在炎热干燥气候下的热性能。采用Crank-Nicolson有限差分法求解瞬态热方程，并通过物理信息神经算子（PINO）学习参数到解的映射。结果表明，粘土-秸秆土坯在标称条件下具有最佳性价比，PINO在数据稀缺时优势显著，并发现了热排斥与热传导两种机制的分界点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在炎热干燥气候区（夏季温度常超45°C），低收入农村住房需要低成本本土建筑材料以减少热量通过墙体渗透，从而保障室内热舒适。现有研究缺乏对多种低成本本土材料的热性能系统比较和优化选择方法。

Method: 采用两阶段计算框架：第一阶段使用验证的Crank-Nicolson有限差分法求解一维瞬态热方程，生成1500个周期解；第二阶段构建以傅里叶神经算子为骨干的物理信息神经算子（PINO），同时满足数据保真度和PDE一致性，学习参数到解的映射。

Result: PINO的相对L2场误差为5.14e-4，峰值内表面温度平均绝对误差为0.201 K，准确复现FDM的材料排名。PINO用150个样本即达到数据驱动FNO用300个样本的性能。ISO 13786时间延迟和衰减因子的误差分别小于0.99小时和0.010。粘土-秸秆土坯性价比最优。气候扫描发现热排斥/传导两种机制的分界点。

Conclusion: 该框架为炎热干燥地区灾后重建中的材料选择提供了基于证据的决策支持，PINO在数据稀缺场景下兼具效率和精度，粘土-秸秆土坯是标称条件下的最佳选择。

Abstract: Identifying cost-effective indigenous building materials that minimise heat penetration through walls is critical for indoor thermal comfort in low-income rural housing in hot-dry climates, where summer temperatures routinely exceed 45 C. We present a two-stage computational framework for thermal ranking of five low-cost indigenous wall materials: mud brick, clay-straw adobe, lime-stabilised bamboo panel, fired clay brick, and lime-mud composite. First, a validated Crank-Nicolson finite difference method (FDM) solves the one-dimensional transient heat equation with Robin boundary conditions under diurnal solar and outdoor air-temperature forcing, generating 1500 periodic-day solutions across a nine-dimensional parameter space by Latin Hypercube sampling. Second, a Physics-Informed Neural Operator (PINO) with a Fourier Neural Operator (FNO) backbone learns the parameter-to-solution operator mu -> T(x,t), enforcing both data fidelity and PDE consistency. The trained PINO attains a relative L2 field error of 5.14e-4 and a 0.201 K mean absolute error on the peak inner surface temperature, preserving the FDM material ranking exactly; PINO trained on 150 FDM samples matches a data-only FNO trained on twice as many, so the physics loss is most valuable when data are scarce. The periodic-day formulation also yields the ISO 13786 time lag and decrement factor, reproduced to within 0.99 h and 0.010. At nominal hot-dry summer conditions, clay-straw adobe achieves the best cost-performance index among widely available materials. A climate sweep, confirmed by FDM spot checks, reveals a regime boundary: under sub-ambient outdoor conditions the ranking inverts to conductive fired clay brick, delineating heat-exclusion and heat-rejection regimes. The framework supports evidence-based material selection for post-flood reconstruction in hot-dry regions.

</details>


### [138] [Rashomon Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25680)
*Moisés Santos,Peter van der Putten,Bernhard Pfahringer,Carlos Soares*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Rashomon对齐（RA）方法，从几何视角衡量模型间功能相似性，超越传统基于数据分布的度量，在完整数据空间中评估决策边界对齐程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有功能相似性度量仅依赖于真实数据分布，只能评估输入空间中已有数据区域内的模型差异，缺乏对整个数据空间（包括无数据区域）的全面视角。

Method: 引入几何视角的Rashomon对齐度量，通过在实例空间中均匀采样数据来计算模型的几何相似性，提出几何Rashomon对齐（Geometric RA）作为衡量指标。

Result: 在90多个数据集上的实验表明，几何对齐与分布对齐提供了互补的视角，且在模型对齐与预测准确性出现分歧的关键案例中表现显著。

Conclusion: RA可用于模型选择、集成构建以及增强机器学习模型与算法的可解释性，提供更全面的模型相似性评估方法。

Abstract: We propose Rashomon Alignment (RA), a new measure to assess functional similarity between two models. Existing functional similarity measures are distributional, quantifying differences between outputs of models applied to real-world data. However, these measures can be regarded as ecologically valid only for regions in the input space represented by the available data. We introduce a geometrical perspective on functional model similarity, which estimates it across the entire data space, offering a comprehensive view of decision boundary alignment independent of any specific data distribution. We also propose geometric Rashomon Alignment as a measure of geometrical similarity, which is computed using data uniformly sampled from the instance space. We perform an experimental analysis on more than 90 datasets, examining critical cases where model alignment diverges from predictive accuracy. Our results show that geometrical and distributional alignment provide different and complementary perspectives on the similarity between models and algorithms. RA can be used for multiple purposes, including model selection, ensemble construction, and enhanced interpretability of machine learning models and algorithms.

</details>


### [139] [From Deterministic to Generative Deep Learning for Urban Air Quality Reconstruction from Sparse Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25687)
*Abhishek A. Sabnis,Mihai Mitrea,Lya Lugon,Karine Sartelet,Marc Bocquet,Xiaoyuan Cheng,Shupeng Zhu,Sibo Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于扩散模型的生成式框架，利用深度学习从稀疏观测数据中重建NO2、O3、PM2.5和PM10四种空气污染物的全空间场，在巴黎实际数据上验证了模型的有效性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空气污染的全场重建对污染暴露评估和公共卫生决策至关重要，但污染物间复杂交互、难以预测的天气模式以及有限的监测站点覆盖使得这一任务极具挑战性。

Method: 采用基于扩散模型的生成式框架进行多污染物重建，使用全场模拟数据训练模型，并引入数据增强方法使模型无需重新训练即可迁移到实际观测数据。

Result: 在模拟验证数据上取得高结构相似度，在实际观测数据上生成符合真实空间模式的结果（通过功率谱分析验证），且模型可泛化到训练周期之外。

Conclusion: 深度学习模型在空气污染重建任务中具有可靠的实际部署潜力，能够从稀疏观测中快速准确地重建污染全场面貌。

Abstract: Full-field reconstruction of air pollution is essential for evaluating pollution exposure and supporting public health decision-making. However, the complex interactions among pollutants, hard-to-predict weather patterns, and limited monitoring station coverage make this a complex task. We apply deep learning techniques to provide fast and accurate reconstructions from sparse observations of four key pollutants: NO2, O3, PM2.5 and PM10. Models are trained on full-field simulation data and evaluated on real-world observations collected from 9 to 28 monitoring stations in the city of Paris. We introduce a diffusion-based generative framework for multi-pollutant reconstruction and benchmark its performance against deterministic deep learning models. Despite noisy observations and strong spatial variability, the models achieve high structural similarity on simulated validation data and produce realistic spatial patterns on real-world observations, as indicated by power-spectrum analysis. We introduce data augmentation methods that enable transfer to real-world observations without retraining, allowing the models to generalise beyond the training period. These findings highlight the potential of ML models for reliable real-world deployment in air pollution reconstruction tasks.

</details>


### [140] [Optimization with Dynamic Constraint Learning (DCL)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25719)
*Ezgi Oztekin,Figen Oztoprak,S. Ilker Birbil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出动态约束学习（DCL）框架，在约束函数未知且无法查询的情况下，通过局部代理模型和数据支撑的信任区域进行约束优化，实现接近全局模型的解质量，同时使用更简单的局部模型和更小的子问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决当约束函数未知且无法在优化过程中查询时，如何进行有效约束优化的问题。

Method: 每次迭代中，从邻近数据学习局部代理模型，并在数据支撑的信任区域内求解子问题。

Result: 在合成测试问题和两个文献案例中验证了DCL的性能，能够达到与全局模型相当的解质量。

Conclusion: DCL使用局部代理模型替代全局模型，在降低模型复杂度和子问题规模的同时，仍能保持良好优化性能。

Abstract: We propose Dynamic Constraint Learning (DCL), a data-driven framework for constrained optimization when constraint functions are unknown and cannot be queried during optimization. At each iteration, the method learns a local surrogate from nearby data and solves a subproblem within a data-supported trust region. Compared with offline global constraint learning, the approach uses local surrogates that adapt to the data distribution during optimization and can achieve solution quality comparable to that of global models while using simpler local models and smaller optimization subproblems. We demonstrate the performance of DCL on a synthetic test problem and two case studies from the literature.

</details>


### [141] [Detecting CSAM Text-to-Image LoRAs From Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25750)
*David Demitri Africa,Cate Heine,Nadine Staes-Polet,Kimberly Mai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过分析LoRA权重中的奇异向量（尤其是u₁），无需生成输出即可检测其是否针对儿童性虐待材料（CSAM）等有害内容进行微调，该方法具有鲁棒性和跨模型泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于元数据或生成输出的审核方式存在缺陷：元数据可被篡改，而生成CSAM输出本身可能违法或不可接受。因此需要一种直接从模型权重检测有害LoRA的无推断方法。

Method: 利用LoRA更新矩阵的左上奇异向量（u₁）作为指纹：该向量编码了LoRA学习到的最强变化，通过分析该指纹即可判断训练数据是否包含特定内容（以年龄作为CSAM的良性代理）。

Result: u₁指纹能够准确识别LoRA的训练内容，可泛化到不同基座模型，对无关良性内容能正确弃权，且对权重噪声、缩放和精度降低具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 有害LoRA可以直接通过权重筛查，无需依赖元数据或生成有害输出，为模型分发前的安全审核提供了新的可行性方案。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) fine-tuning has made it cheap and easy to customize open-weight image generation models for specific tasks, including the production of child sexual abuse material (CSAM). Existing moderation relies on metadata or generated outputs, but metadata can be deceptive and generating outputs may itself be unacceptable or illegal. We show that a safer signal lives in the weights. The top-left singular vectors of a LoRA's updates form a compact, inference-free fingerprint ($u_1$) of its strongest learned change. Using human-subject age as a benign proxy for CSAM, we find that $u_1$ identifies what a LoRA was trained on, generalizes across base models, and abstains on unrelated benign content. The signal is robust to additive weight noise, rescaling, and precision reduction. These results indicate that harmful LoRAs could be screened directly from their weights without relying on metadata or generating harmful outputs.

</details>


### [142] [An Embarrassingly Simple Rule-based Visiting Circulation Approach to Trip Destination Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25751)
*Eng-Shen Tu,Yong-Han Chen,En-Chao Liu,Hao-Yun Keng,Cheng-Te Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于规则的访问循环（RVC）模型，用于解决IEEE Big Data Cup 2022中的出行目的地预测挑战。该方法利用出行者的重访行为和出发地-目的地关系，无需从训练区域学习即可预测目标区域的出行目的地，最终在比赛中获得第二名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决目标 metropolitan area 目的地完全未知的出行目的地预测挑战，传统监督学习方法在此场景下失效。

Method: 提出基于规则的访问循环（RVC）模型，直接利用出发地信息和个体出行行为（重访行为及出发地-目的地关系）来预测目标区域的目的地，无需从四个训练区域进行学习。

Result: 在离线评估和排行榜提交中，RVC方法均显著优于监督学习方法和其他启发式方法，最终在比赛中获得第二名。

Conclusion: 重访行为及出发地-目的地关系在个体出行中起关键作用，基于规则的简单方法在零样本目的地预测任务中比复杂的监督学习方法更有效。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose the Rule-based Visiting Circulation (RVC) model in tackling the challenge in the IEEE Big Data Cup 2022: Trip Destination Prediction. Given trips containing travel information, personal attributes, origin zones, and their features in the training metropolitan areas, the task is to predict the destination of every trip in a targeted metropolitan area whose destinations are not given at all at the training stage. We highlight the challenges in this destination prediction task -- having no knowledge of the destinations in the targeted metropolitan area. We provide insights from the datasets, in which revisiting behaviors and the relationships between origins and destinations play a crucial role in individuals' trips. Hence, we design a simple but comprehensive method, rule-based visiting circulation, which directly utilizes the origin information and individuals' trip behaviors to determine the destinations in the targeted metropolitan area, i.e., requiring no learning from the four training areas. Experimental results on both offline evaluation and leaderboard submission consistently exhibit the proposed RVC can significantly outperform supervised learning methods and other heuristics. The RVC method eventually brings us to second place in the competition leaderboard.

</details>


### [143] [SpectONet: A Physics-Guided Spectral Deep Operator Network for Euler-Bernoulli Beam Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25790)
*Shivani Saini,Ramesh Kumar Vats,Arup Kumar Sahoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SpectONet，一种结合物理约束与切比雪夫-高斯-洛巴托（CGL）非均匀传感器布置的谱深度算子网络，用于求解欧拉-伯努利梁振动问题，在合成数据和真实桥梁振动数据上均显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DeepONet采用均匀传感器分布，难以有效捕捉边界敏感的结构响应，导致预测精度受限。本文旨在通过非均匀谱传感器布置和物理约束提升算子网络在结构振动分析中的性能。

Method: 将DeepONet的算子学习能力与物理信息约束（控制方程、初始/边界条件）相结合，并采用CGL非均匀传感器布置策略，在边界附近密集采样以提高有限维表示的准确性。

Result: 在三个合成EBB振动问题和真实桥梁振动数据集上，SpectONet相比Vanilla DeepONet、PI-DeepONet、PINN和CNN-UNet等基线模型，合成问题误差降低至少64%，真实问题降低至少37%。

Conclusion: SpectONet提供了一种精确、计算高效且物理一致的算子学习框架，适用于结构振动分析，验证了非均匀传感器布置和物理约束相结合的有效性。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel physics-guided spectral deep operator network, termed SpectONet, for solving Euler-Bernoulli beam (EBB) vibration problems. The proposed framework integrates the operator-learning capability of DeepONet with physics-informed constraints and Chebyshev-Gauss-Lobatto (CGL) sensor placement. Unlike conventional DeepONet frameworks, which commonly employ uniformly distributed sensors, SpectONet uses nonuniform spectral sensor locations with a higher concentration of points near the domain boundaries. This sampling strategy improves the finite-dimensional representation of boundary-sensitive structural responses while requiring only a limited number of branch-network inputs. The governing beam equation, together with the associated initial and boundary conditions, incorporated into the training objective to promote physically consistent and generalizable predictions. Numerical experiments on three synthetic EBB vibration problems and a real-world bridge vibration dataset demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework. Comparisons with strong baselines such as, Vanilla DeepONet, PI-DeepONet, PINN, and CNN-UNet show that SpectONet consistently achieves lower prediction errors across all considered evaluation metrics. In particular, SpectONet achieves at least \(64\%\) improvement over the considered baseline models across the three synthetic problems and at least \(37\%\) for the real-world problems. These results demonstrate that SpectONet provides an accurate, computationally efficient, and physically consistent operator-learning framework for structural vibration analysis.

</details>


### [144] [Prototype Adaptation for Zero-Shot sEMG Movement Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25826)
*Rui Liu,Benjamin Paassen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出两种新方法CPI和SAP，使原型网络能在仅训练基本动作的情况下，零样本识别未见过的组合动作，在sEMG假肢控制中准确率提升超20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日常活动需要组合动作，但收集所有组合动作的训练数据耗时且需重新训练模型。

Method: 提出Compositional Prototype Interpolation (CPI)和Synthetic Adaptation for Prototypes (SAP)，基于嵌入空间的线性插值假设，实现零样本学习组合动作。

Result: 在NearLab、NinaPro DB3和新采BasCom数据集上，SAP方法相较以往零样本学习方法在组合动作识别上准确率提升超20%。

Conclusion: SAP方法在离线实验和在线用户研究中均表现优异，有效解决了组合动作零样本识别难题。

Abstract: Surface electromyography (sEMG) enables the control of prostheses, allowing upper-limb amputees to re-gain some hand function. Most current research focuses on recognizing basic movements for prosthesis control. However, in most daily activities, such as opening a door, combined movements are essential. However, collecting training data for all possible combined movements is time-consuming and requires re-training of the model for any new combination. We propose two novel recognition approaches, Compositional Prototype Interpolation (CPI) and Synthetic Adaptation for Prototypes (SAP), that enable zero-shot learning of combined, novel and unseen movements in Prototype Networks after training only with basic movements. Our methods rest on a linear interpolation assumption in the embedding space, which we study by inspecting the geometry of combined motions in signal and embedding space. In experiments on the NearLab and NinaPro DB3 data sets as well as our newly recorded BasCom dataset, our proposed SAP outperforms prior zero-shot learning methods with accuracy improvements on combined movements of more than 20%. This advantage is maintained in online inference experiments in a user study.

</details>


### [145] [DRIFT: Direct-Recursive Intervention-Conditioned Forecasting of ICU Physiological Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25864)
*Weixin Liu,Juming Xiong,Congning Ni,Yanfan Zhu,Xingtao Lin,Bradley A. Malin,Zhijun Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DRIFT是一个混合预测框架，结合直接预测和递归动作条件校正，用于ICU时间序列预测，在MIMIC-IV和eICU-CRD数据集上相比TFT-action实现了轻微但稳健的MAP误差降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列预测模型要么直接预测全部未来序列而难以利用治疗动作信息，要么自回归模型会累积误差，因此需要一种能有效结合治疗动作信息且减少误差累积的预测框架。

Method: DRIFT采用混合框架：直接模型产生主预测，递归的、以动作为条件的模型贡献受约束的校正。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV和eICU-CRD数据集上，DRIFT在8、24、48小时预测端点上平均MAP MAE降低0.673%；在治疗序列改变的子集上8和24小时误差更低；鲁棒性实验中优势持续存在。

Conclusion: DRIFT在整体精度提升适度的情况下，在治疗序列变化的场景下表现出更优的预测性能，验证了混合框架在ICU时间序列预测中的有效性。

Abstract: Many time-series forecasts depend not only on prior observations but also on actions specified during the forecast period. In intensive care units (ICUs), future vital signs and laboratory values are influenced by treatments such as vasopressors. However, models that predict the full future sequence all at once make little use of these treatments, whereas autoregressive models can accumulate errors. We introduce DRIFT, a hybrid framework in which a direct model produces the primary forecast and a recursive, action-conditioned model contributes constrained corrections. We evaluate DRIFT on 6,046 admissions from MIMIC-IV and 8,345 admissions from eICU-CRD. Averaged across the 8-, 24-, and 48-hour forecast endpoints, DRIFT reduces mean absolute error for mean arterial pressure (MAP) by 0.673% relative to an action-conditioned Temporal Fusion Transformer (TFT-action) on MIMIC-IV and achieves the lowest corresponding error among the compared models on eICU-CRD. Although the overall accuracy improvement is modest, a MIMIC-IV audit restricted to windows in which the supplied treatment sequence was altered showed that DRIFT achieved lower observed-target MAP error than TFT-action at 8 and 24 hours. Treatment-sequence alteration increased DRIFT's MAP error by 0.21-0.26 mmHg more than it increased TFT-action's error, with prediction changes occurring primarily after the supplied paths diverged. In a separate robustness experiment, the MAP advantage persisted under three shared checkpoint-selection rules emphasizing overall endpoint error, MAP error, or both equally.

</details>


### [146] [A2TTA: Anchored-and-Agile Test-Time Adaptation for Evolving Traffic Sensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25875)
*Du Yin,Xiachong Lin,Yue Tan,Jinliang Deng,Estrid He,Hao Xue,Flora D. Salim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出A2TTA框架，通过锚定-敏捷测试时适应方法，解决交通预测中动态拓扑演变和多尺度时间偏移问题，在十个真实网络数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通预测研究通常假设传感器图固定不变，忽略了真实交通网络（如道路建设、人类移动模式演变）的持续演化，导致传统模型性能严重下降。

Method: 提出A2TTA框架：将拓扑演变导致的预测误差转化为可扩展的输出校准问题；将时间适应分解为持久全局校正和敏捷上下文特定特化，联合处理拓扑演化与多尺度时间偏移。

Result: 在十个真实交通网络上进行大量实验，A2TTA在不同骨干网络、数据集和预测时长下均一致提升了预测性能。

Conclusion: A2TTA能够高效、鲁棒地适应持续演变的交通环境，为动态交通网络测试时适应提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Traffic forecasting is important for efficient traffic management and route planning in smart cities. Existing traffic forecasting studies typically assume fixed sensor graphs, overlooking the continuous evolution of real-world traffic networks, e.g., ongoing road network construction and evolving human mobility patterns. These dynamic changes can substantially degrade conventional forecasting models, motivating test-time adaptation (TTA) to efficiently adapt pretrained models during deployment. However, applying TTA to evolving traffic sensor networks remains challenging in two aspects. First, topology expansion introduces new sensors and connections, continuously reshaping the sensor graph. Second, tem- poral shifts vary in time scale and stability, requiring differentiated adaptation to long-term and short-term shifts. In this study, we address these challenges by proposing A2TTA, an Anchored-and-Agile Test-Time Adaptation framework for evolving traffic sensor networks, which transforms topology-induced forecasting errors into an expandable output calibration problem and separates tem- poral adaptation into persistent global correction and agile context-specific specialization. By jointly addressing topology evolution and multi-scale temporal shifts, A2TTA enables efficient and robust adaptation to continuously evolving traffic environments. Extensive experiments on ten real-world traffic networks demonstrate that A2TTA consistently improves forecasting performance across different backbones, datasets, and prediction horizons. Our code is available in https://github.com/lixus7/A2TTA.

</details>


### [147] [Minimizing Targeted Activations: Input-Only Suppression of Evaluation-Awareness Latents in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25907)
*Deepanshu Mody,Samarth Agarwal,Utkarsh Mittal,Dipesh Mahato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过优化输入提示来抑制模型内部潜在表征（评估意识相关）的方法，发现虽然可以成功抑制这些潜在表征，但激活可读性不等于行为可控性，CAA方向存在安慰剂效应，且抑制评价方向无法降低模型的行为评估判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如果模型在检测到被测试时表现出不同行为，将威胁安全评估的有效性。本文旨在研究能否通过输入侧优化来抑制与"评估意识"相关的内部潜在表征，从而控制模型行为。

Method: 采用Fluent Dreaming/EPO方法，结合GCG风格令牌优化和自交叉熵流畅性正则化，在Llama-3.2-3B和Llama-3.1-8B上抑制五种目标构建（CAA方向、子空间范数、SAE特征、单个MLP神经元、行为logit）下的潜在表征。

Result: 潜在表征可以被稳健抑制（z≈-7），SAE特征可被完全关闭。但CAA方向存在安慰剂效应：随机方向同样被抑制且同样改变行为；在真实评估上下文中，抑制评价方向反而略微增加了模型的评估判断；单个MLP神经元与评估相关但不具有因果性。

Conclusion: 激活可读性不等于行为可控性，内部方向的可读性不能保证其行为控制能力，对安全评估方法提出了警示。

Abstract: Activation steering controls model behavior by editing internal activations at inference time. We study its input-side dual: optimizing a fluent prompt so that a chosen internal latent is driven toward zero, with no inference-time model access. Our target is an "evaluation-awareness" latent-linearly readable and steerable in recent work-whose control would threaten the validity of safety evaluations if models behave differently when they detect being tested. Adapting Fluent Dreaming / EPO with a negated feature term (GCG-style token optimization plus a self-cross-entropy fluency regularizer, swept over a fluency weight), we suppress the latent under five target constructions-a CAA direction, a subspace norm, an SAE feature, a single MLP neuron, and a behavioral logit-on Llama-3.2-3B and Llama-3.1-8B. The latent is robustly suppressible ($z\approx-7$), and a causally-validated Llama Scope SAE feature can be fully and selectively turned off. But our controls tell a cautionary story about the CAA direction: a placebo random direction is suppressed just as hard and shifts behavior just as far, and when we hold a real eval passage in context and optimize only a prefix, suppressing the eval-direction fails to reduce-and slightly increases-the model's behavioral eval judgment. Activation-readability, in short, is not behavioral controllability. We further find that a single MLP neuron is eval-correlated but not causal at both scales, and that scanning the real Pile yields a natural-text baseline competitive with the optimizer for the internal direction. A positive control validates our erasure detector, bounding an erasure-vs-rotation question earlier left open.

</details>


### [148] [Reinforcement Learning for Code Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25970)
*Pierre Chambon,Kunhao Zheng,Juliette Decugis,Benoit Sagot,Gabriel Synnaeve*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出三阶段方法（DMC-Optim测试集、速度奖励设计、GRPO自适应训练）解决代码优化RL中执行时间噪声大、奖励稀疏、训练不稳定等问题，在多个基准上显著提升优化型代码生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL for code correctness方法成熟，但扩展至代码优化时，将执行时间加入奖励会导致测量噪声、奖励稀疏、GRPO不稳定等问题，使RL失效。

Method: 三阶段方法：(1)构建DMC-Optim大规模优化测试集与校准沙箱；(2)在RL环境中组合正确性与速度奖励，用离线模拟器预测最优配置；(3)针对稀疏、带噪声的定时执行环境，调整GRPO与评估策略。

Result: DMC-Optim上top-50% pass@1：Qwen 2.5 7B从18.0%提升至31.3%，CWM 32B从30.7%提升至50.4%；top-30%相对提升125%；退化沙箱下提升100%-200%；LCB上速度比较胜率达83%。

Conclusion: 所提方法显著提升了代码优化RL的有效性，在保持纯正确性分数的同时大幅提高优化性能，但相对于人类专家的复杂类改进率仍有差距（14% vs. 28%）。

Abstract: RL for code correctness is now established: have the model generate a program, run it against hidden test cases, and reward solutions that pass. Extending this to code optimization seems straightforward: just add execution time to the reward. But in practice, once timing drives the reward, small problems in measurement noise, reward sparsity, or GRPO instability overwhelm the signal and make RL fail: generated solutions are barely faster, and more of them can fail. We make execution time learnable through three stages: (1) how code is tested, by building DMC-Optim with large optimization tests and a calibrated sandbox; (2) how speed is turned into reward, by composing correctness and speed in the RL environment and using an offline simulator to predict the most promising configurations; and (3) how the model learns from that reward, by adapting GRPO and evaluation to the sparser, noisier timed-execution setting. On DMC-Optim, the strongest optimization-aware configurations improve strict top-50% pass@1 from 18.0% to 31.3% on Qwen 2.5 7B and from 30.7% to 50.4% on CWM 32B. These gains further increase at stricter percentiles such as top-30%, with 125% relative improvement for CWM 32B, while preserving pure-correctness scores. When the timing sandbox is degraded, robust optimization RL reaches 100% to 200% improvement over standard RLVR, depending on the evaluation criterion. On LCB, CWM 32B wins up to 83% of median-sample speed comparisons against standard RLVR. Relative to the fastest correct human submissions per problem, it reaches about half the human rate of complexity-class improvements (14% vs. 28%).

</details>


### [149] [Generator-Aligned Representation Interfaces for Diagnostic Soft Equivariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25988)
*Weitao Li,Gong Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GARI是一种表示级设计原则，通过对齐规范视图和生成器诱导视图，将选择的变换生成器暴露给通用序列骨干网络，在不需特定群组重设计的情况下实现任务相关的变换一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的精确等变架构通常需要专门的算子编码特定群作用，导致难以跨骨干网络和数据模态复用。作者希望提出一种表示级的设计原则，使通用序列骨干网络能够访问和利用变换生成器信息。

Method: 提出Generator-Aligned Representation Interface (GARI)，通过构建生成器索引流、转换为公共交互帧、共享参数处理、修复顺序导致的上下文不匹配、实现跨流信息交换，并利用流间差异进行聚合。引入Direct Equivariance Error (DEE)作为冻结检查点的诊断指标。

Result: 在基因组序列、图像和三维点云三个模态上验证了方法有效性，涵盖序列反转、平面旋转反射和受控轴向变换等任务，无需针对特定群组重新设计序列骨干网络。

Conclusion: GARI为硬等变架构提供了可移植的诊断补充，使生成器结构可访问、可学习、可测量，同时有限探针证据与连续群上精确等变的认证保持区别。

Abstract: Exact-equivariant architectures typically encode prescribed group actions in specialized operators, which can complicate their reuse with generic backbones and across data modalities. We introduce the Generator-Aligned Representation Interface (GARI), a representation-level design principle that exposes selected transformation generators to a generic sequence backbone through aligned canonical and generator-induced views. We formalize the resulting behavior using a probe-specific soft-equivariance residual defined over declared data and transformation distributions. This framework distinguishes representation consistency from task robustness and exact equivariance, and localizes residual mismatch to interface construction, shared stream processing, and terminal fusion. We instantiate the interface as GARI-Net, which constructs generator-indexed streams, converts them into a common interaction frame, processes them with shared parameters, repairs ordering-induced context mismatch, enables cross-stream information exchange, and aggregates them using inter-stream discrepancy. Direct Equivariance Error (DEE) provides a frozen-checkpoint diagnostic of the prescribed representation relation under known token or voxel actions. Experiments on genomic sequences, images, and three-dimensional point clouds examine sequence reversal, planar rotations and reflections, and controlled axial transfer. Across these settings, the same interface principle supports task-relevant transformation consistency and generalization to declared held-out probes without requiring group-specific redesign of the sequence backbone. GARI therefore provides a portable diagnostic complement to hard-equivariant architectures: it makes generator structure accessible, learnable, and measurable, while finite-probe evidence remains distinct from certification of exact equivariance over a continuous group.

</details>


### [150] [Empirical Evaluation of Out-Of-Distribution Performance of Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26000)
*Malena Loza,David Chushig-Muzo,Eva Milara,Luis Bote-Curiel,Luis Estrada-Petrocelli,Felipe Grijalva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了9种表格基础模型（TFMs）在分布偏移（OOD）下的表现，发现所有模型均出现性能下降，偏移差距在0.003-0.060之间，且高性能模型存在可扩展性瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TFMs大多在独立同分布数据上训练和评估，但现实场景中常存在分布偏移，影响模型鲁棒性，而针对TFMs在分布偏移下的研究十分有限。

Method: 选取9种涵盖不同预训练策略和架构的TFMs，在HELOC、Voting、Childhood Lead三个真实数据集上（覆盖标签、社会经济、地理三种偏移类型）进行OOD性能实证评估。

Result: 所有TFMs在分布偏移下均系统性退化，偏移差距为0.003-0.060；经典表格模型的ID与OOD性能关系同样适用于TFMs；高性能模型存在显著的可扩展性差距。

Conclusion: 该研究扩展了OOD表格数据基准，为TFMs在存在结构性分布偏移的高风险场景中的应用提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs) have emerged as novel approaches for tabular predictive tasks, demonstrating competitive predictive performance to ensemble tree-based models. Most TFMs are trained and evaluated on independent and identically distributed data, but this assumption changes in real-world scenarios due to distribution shifts, which compromise the robustness of models. Limited research has been conducted of TFMs under distribution shifts. We present an empirical evaluation of Out-Of-Distribution (OOD) performance of nine TFMs, spanning diverse pre-training strategies and architectures: TabPFNv2, TabPFNv2.5, TabPFNv2.6, TabPFNv3, TabICL, TabICLv2, Mitra, LimiX and TabFM. Three real-world datasets from the TableShift study were considered (HELOC, Voting, Childhood Lead), covering label, socioeconomic, and geographic shift types. Our results show that all evaluated TFMs degrade systematically under distribution shift regardless of pre-training strategy, with shift gaps ranging from 0.003 to 0.060 depending on shift type. The relationship between in-distribution and OOD predictive performance documented for classical tabular models extends into TFMs. We also identified a scalability gap, as high-performing models demand significant memory and computational resources beyond what standard deployment infrastructure can support. This study extends existing benchmarks for OOD in tabular data, providing evidence to support their adoption in high-stakes domains characterized by structural distribution shifts.

</details>


### [151] [Sharpness-Aware Minimization and Muon: Robustness under the Spectral Norm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26001)
*Wenzhi Zhong,Edward Milsom,Michael Murray*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出矩阵感知几何的锐度感知最小化方法（SAM），在内部扰动中使用逐层谱内扰动，外部更新结合Muon优化器，在ImageNet-1K上对ViT-Small/16和ResNet-50取得最佳验证准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAM变体对"小扰动"的几何定义缺乏统一视角，哪种几何最有效尚不明确。受矩阵感知优化（如Muon）启发，希望将矩阵结构引入SAM的几何设计中。

Method: 提出层间谱内扰动用于矩阵值隐藏层参数，在内部步骤中考虑矩阵结构的谱几何；外部步骤结合AdamW/SGDW或Muon优化器进行更新。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K上对ViT-Small/16和ResNet-50进行实验，谱内扰动+Muon外更新的组合在所有评估方法中取得最佳验证准确率。

Conclusion: 矩阵感知的谱几何在SAM中效果显著，与Muon优化器结合能进一步提升泛化性能，为优化器的几何设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM) aims to improve generalization by encouraging insensitivity to small, worst-case parameter perturbations. However, the notion of a "small" perturbation is inherently geometry-dependent: while existing SAM variants have explored a wide range of choices, a clear perspective on which geometries are most effective in practice remains elusive. Recent work on matrix-aware optimization, particularly the Muon optimizer, suggests that respecting the matrix structure of hidden-layer weights can lead to strong empirical performance. Motivated by this, we study matrix-aware geometry in both stages of SAM: we introduce a layerwise spectral inner perturbation for matrix-valued hidden-layer parameters and combine it with either AdamW/SGDW or Muon in the outer update. Across ImageNet-1K experiments on ViT-Small/16 and ResNet-50, we find that the combination of a spectral inner step with a Muon outer step performs consistently strongly, achieving the best validation accuracy on both models among the evaluated methods.

</details>


### [152] [Reinformed Dreamer: An Asymmetric World Model Efficiently Trained through Latent Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26040)
*Gaspard Lambrechts,Adrien Bolland,Daniel Ebi,Damien Ernst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究非对称强化学习在模型-based方法中的效果，发现Informed Dreamer在特权信息表示上存在局限，并提出Reinformed Dreamer算法，通过潜在引导改进表示学习，实验表明其优于先前方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习可从额外监督信息中受益，类似人类学习需要引导。现有非对称强化学习方法在部分可观测和完全可观测场景下有效，但模型-based方法中特权信息表示存在局限，需改进。

Method: 提出Reinformed Dreamer算法，采用潜在引导（latent guidance）作为新的非对称表示学习目标，改进模型-based强化学习中的特权信息表示。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，Reinformed Dreamer相比Dreamer的提升比先前非对称方法更稳定且一致。

Conclusion: 非对称表示学习目标使用潜在引导能有效改善模型-based强化学习中的特权信息表示，带来更稳定的性能提升。

Abstract: Much like humans benefit from guidance while learning, reinforcement learning algorithms may benefit from additional supervision beyond rewards. Leveraging additional information during training to learn better representations and behaviors has been the focus of asymmetric reinforcement learning. This learning paradigm has proven effective under partial observability when additional state information is available, but also under full observability when more refined state information is available. Focusing on model-based reinforcement learning, we study the effect of asymmetric learning on observation representations and on privileged information representations. First, we identify a limitation in the privileged information representations learned by an asymmetric model-based algorithm known as the Informed Dreamer. Then, we propose a novel asymmetric representation learning objective using latent guidance, resulting in a new algorithm called the Reinformed Dreamer. Experiments across several benchmarks show a more consistent improvement over Dreamer than previous asymmetric approaches.

</details>


### [153] [Re-thinking Mammography Transfer Learning: The Dataset-Informed Transfer Learning (DITL) Framework for Breast Cancer Screening and Lesion Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26043)
*Adarsh Bhandary Panambur,Siming Bayer,Andreas Maier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出数据集引导的迁移学习框架(DITL)，通过自适应难度加权交叉熵(A-DWCE)和自适应邻域表示三元组(A-NR-Triplet)两个创新组件，在无需超参数调优的情况下，在大型乳腺X线摄影数据集和小型ROI数据集上均取得了显著的分类性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统迁移学习方法忽略数据集特定特征，而现有的邻域信息方法局限于狭窄任务且公式僵化，难以扩展至大规模人群级数据集，因此需要一种可扩展、鲁棒且无需超参数调优的迁移学习框架。

Method: DITL框架包含两个自适应组件：1) A-DWCE，基于自监督特征空间中的k近邻标签纯度分配样本权重；2) A-NR-Triplet，利用可学习边界增强类内紧凑性和类间分离性。两组件整合到统一目标函数中，无需超参数调优和计算开销。

Result: 在VinDR-Mammo大型数据集上，DITL在全图乳腺密度分类任务中达到了最先进水平，在准确率、F1分数和AUC上均有显著提升(p<0.0001)；在小型ROI数据集上也取得了一致且统计显著的性能提升(p<0.0001)。

Conclusion: DITL通过桥接小型病变分析与大规模密度估计，建立了一个临床相关、可扩展且通用的乳腺X线摄影分类框架，覆盖了从乳腺癌筛查到诊断的完整谱系。

Abstract: Enhancing classification performance in mammography remains a persistent challenge across both small curated datasets and large-scale clinical cohorts. Conventional transfer learning approaches often neglect dataset-specific characteristics, while recent neighborhood-informed methods have been restricted to narrow tasks with rigid formulations, limiting their scalability to population-level datasets. To address these challenges, we propose the Dataset-Informed Transfer Learning (DITL) framework, which integrates dataset-derived difficulty signals with neighborhood-based triplet supervision in a unified objective. DITL introduces two adaptive components: (i) Adaptive Difficulty-Weighted Cross-Entropy (A-DWCE), which assigns per-sample weights based on k-nearest neighbor label purity in a self-supervised feature space, and (ii) Adaptive Neighborhood Representation Triplet (A-NR-Triplet), which enforces intra-class compactness and inter-class separation using a learnable margin. Unlike focal loss, DITL requires no hyperparameter tuning, removes heuristic weighting and fixed margins, and incurs negligible computational overhead, yielding a robust and scalable optimization strategy. On the large-scale VinDR-Mammo dataset, DITL achieves state-of-the-art performance for whole-image breast density classification, with significant improvements across accuracy, F1-score, and AUC (p < 0.0001). Beyond large cohorts, DITL also delivers consistent, statistically significant gains on small ROI datasets (p < 0.0001). By bridging small-scale lesion analysis with large-scale density estimation, DITL establishes a clinically relevant, scalable, and generalizable framework for mammography classification, spanning the full breast cancer screening-to-diagnosis spectrum.

</details>


### [154] [Spend Experts Where You Are Unsure: Confidence-Adaptive Routing for Mixture-of-Experts LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26052)
*Tom Saliencro,Rohan Desai,Priya Nair,Maya Lindqvist,Daniel Whitmore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CARE是一种置信度自适应专家路由方法，根据每个token的模型不确定性动态调整激活的专家数量，在保持计算效率的同时提升MoE-LoRA的性能，并可用于分布外检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的MoE-LoRA方法为所有token固定激活k个专家，但不同token的模型不确定性不同，固定k值导致简单token过度计算而困难token计算不足，缺乏自适应机制。

Method: 利用路由器的输出分布作为每个token的不确定性信号，采用核采样方式激活专家：按路由权重降序激活专家直到累积质量达到阈值，当已激活的专家意见不一致时进行少量扩展。引入预算恒温器校准阈值以匹配目标平均活跃专家数。

Result: 在LLaMA-3.1-8B和Qwen2.5-7B的八个常识推理基准、数学、代码和知识任务上，CARE在匹配计算量的情况下优于固定top-k MoE-LoRA，且在激活更少专家的情况下匹配固定k=4基线的性能。同时在分布外检测任务上优于MSP、熵和多轮代理方法。

Conclusion: CARE是一种无需额外参数、单次前向传播的即插即用方法，通过自适应专家路由在效率和性能间取得更好平衡，且专家置信度和分歧信号可有效用于分布外检测。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) variants of Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) route every token to a fixed number of experts $k$. Tokens differ in how uncertain the model is about them, so a single k over-spends on easy tokens and under-serves hard ones. We observe that the router's output distribution is already a per-token uncertainty signal: peaked mass indicates confidence, while a flat distribution indicates ambiguity. We introduce CARE (Confidence-Adaptive Routing of Experts), which admits experts in a nucleus fashion. Experts are activated in decreasing router weight until their cumulative mass reaches a threshold, with a small extension when the admitted experts disagree. A budget thermostat calibrates the threshold so that the average number of active experts matches any target. CARE is a drop-in, single-forward-pass rule with no extra parameters. Across eight commonsense benchmarks on LLaMA-3.1-8B and Qwen2.5-7B, as well as math, code, and knowledge tasks, CARE improves over fixed top-k MoE-LoRA at matched compute and matches the fixed-k=4 baseline while activating fewer experts. The same confidence and disagreement signals also improve out-of-distribution detection over MSP, entropy, and multi-pass proxies. We support the design with nucleus fidelity, budget optimality, and an epistemic reading of disagreement, and we release code.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [155] [Enhancing Error Detection Performance through Parallel CRC Computation on Multi-Core Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24849)
*Mohammad Javad Khani,Mahmood Ahmadi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于POSIX线程的多核架构通用软件并行CRC框架，支持CRC-8/16/32/64/128多种变体，采用GF(2)上的多项式算术和矩阵移位操作确保并行与串行CRC计算的等价性。实验表明，在大数据集上可获得约3-4倍加速比，同时保持CRC计算精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统软件CRC实现存在固有的顺序依赖，难以充分利用现代多核处理器的并行计算能力，亟需一种通用的、可移植的并行CRC加速方案。

Method: 基于POSIX线程的多线程并行框架，采用GF(2)上的CRC组合机制（而非简单XOR聚合），通过多项式算术和矩阵移位操作保证并行与串行计算结果一致。

Result: 在大数据集上实现了约3-4倍的加速效果，同时保证了CRC计算的完全正确性，并提供了与查表法、slicing-by-8、SIMD及硬件辅助CRC等方法的对比分析。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一个可移植、通用的软件方案，能有效在通用多核系统上实现保持正确性的并行CRC加速。

Abstract: Cyclic Redundancy Check (CRC) remains one of the most widely used error-detection mechanisms in communication, storage, and embedded systems. However, conventional software CRC implementations suffer from inherent sequential dependencies that limit efficient utilization of modern multi-core processors. This paper presents a generalized software-based parallel CRC framework for multi-core architectures using POSIX threads. The proposed framework supports multiple CRC variants, including CRC-8, CRC-16, CRC-32, CRC-64, and CRC-128, within a unified implementation model. To preserve correctness during parallel execution, the framework employs a GF(2)-based CRC combination mechanism rather than naive XOR aggregation. The combine stage is formulated using polynomial arithmetic and matrix-based shifting operations over GF(2), ensuring equivalence between parallel and serial CRC computation. The proposed method was evaluated using multiple workload sizes and thread configurations. Experimental analysis includes execution time, throughput, latency, scalability behavior, and energy estimation under varying thread counts. Results indicate that parallel execution significantly improves performance for large datasets, achieving approximately 3-4x speedup on the evaluated platform while preserving exact CRC correctness. Comparative discussion with representative CRC optimization approaches, including lookup-table methods, slicing-by-8, SIMD/vectorized CRC, and hardware-assisted CRC techniques, is also provided to position the proposed framework within the broader CRC optimization landscape. Overall, the proposed approach provides a portable and generalized software framework for correctness-preserving parallel CRC acceleration on general-purpose multi-core systems.

</details>


### [156] [Spanergy: Energy-aware Distributed Tracing for Microservices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24902)
*César Perdigão Batista,Denis Conan,Sophie Chabridon*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Spanergy是一种面向微服务应用的能量感知分布式追踪方法，通过关联微服务功率测量与追踪数据，将能耗归因到请求的各个阶段（追踪跨度），在可接受的能耗开销下提供能量效率优化洞见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前微服务应用广泛使用分布式追踪来诊断延迟和故障，但缺乏对单个端用户请求能耗的感知能力，限制了能量感知的调试、问责和控制。

Method: 提出Spanergy方法，将每个微服务的功率测量与分布式追踪数据相关联，将测量的能耗归因到请求的各个分段（追踪跨度），支持同步请求链和异步交互场景。

Result: 启用OpenTelemetry追踪使实验总能耗增加59.1%，Spanergy后处理额外增加15.2%基线能耗；发现部分请求能耗来自延迟关键路径之外的跨度。

Conclusion: 能量感知追踪在适度开销下是可行的，能够为微服务能量效率优化提供可操作的见解。

Abstract: Cloud computing is gaining popularity by giving access to seemingly unlimited virtual resources. However, Cloud data centres are built with physical resources and their electricity consumption has been continuously growing over the past decades. Microservices are an important building block of Cloud applications, calling for new solutions to observe their energy consumption. Distributed tracing is widely deployed to diagnose latency and failures in microservice-based applications, yet it does not expose the energy cost of individual end-user requests. Such a gap limits energy-aware debugging, accountability, and control. This paper presents Spanergy, an energy-aware distributed tracing approach that correlates per-microservice power measurements with traces and that attributes measured energy consumption to request segments, i.e. trace spans. We showcase Spanergy with synchronous request chains and asynchronous interactions across microservices. We present a rigorous experimental protocol and statistical analysis plan to quantify overhead and to validate conservation and coverage properties on realistic configurations. Enabling OpenTelemetry tracing increased total experiment energy by 59.1% relative to the uninstrumented baseline, and Spanergy post-processing added 15.2% of the baseline energy. Hence, Spanergy's incremental energy cost is smaller than the energy overhead of enabling tracing itself, making the approach lightweight in practice. Spanergy also reveals that a non-negligible fraction of request energy comes from spans outside the latency-critical path. These results show that energy-aware tracing is feasible at modest overhead and provides actionable insights for energy-efficient microservices.

</details>


### [157] [QCOEM: Quantum Cloud Orchestration with Evolutionary Multi-Objective Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25358)
*Tam N. Pham,Hoa T. Nguyen,Quan Le-Trung*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出QCOEM框架，利用进化算法（NSGA-II/NSGA-III）进行多目标优化，在异构量子云环境中联合优化任务完成时间、执行错误率和负载均衡，相比噪声无关启发式方法实现约30%的保真度提升和零任务重调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子云编排器使用噪声无关的启发式算法，忽略后端特定错误，导致执行保真度低、负载不均衡和频繁重调度。

Method: 采用NSGA-II和NSGA-III进行多目标优化，使用增强成就标量化函数（AASF）从非凸帕累托前沿选择最优调度方案。

Result: 实现零任务重调度，相比噪声无关启发式方法平均保真度提升约30%，调度开销可控。

Conclusion: QCOEM框架能够为量子云计算提供稳定、高保真度的执行和轻量级资源管理。

Abstract: Quantum cloud platforms need to dynamically orchestrate workloads across heterogeneous quantum computation backends whose noise profiles, qubit topologies, and queues vary over time. Existing orchestrators use noise-agnostic heuristics that ignore backend-specific errors, causing reduced execution fidelity, load imbalance, and frequent rescheduling. To address these challenges, we propose QCOEM - a Quantum Cloud Orchestration framework that leverages Evolutionary algorithms for Multi-objective optimization of quantum task scheduling. We compare NSGA-II and NSGA-III for jointly minimizing mean completion time, execution error rate, and load imbalance. To select schedules from a non-convex Pareto front, we apply an Augmented Achievement Scalarization Function (AASF) as a preference-based decision rule that maps the Pareto set to a single dispatchable schedule aligned with user priorities. Our extensive performance evaluation in a heterogeneous quantum cloud environment shows zero task rescheduling and about 30% higher mean fidelity than noise-agnostic heuristics, while maintaining bounded scheduling overhead. The experiment results indicate that our QCOEM framework can deliver stable, high-fidelity execution and lightweight resource management for quantum cloud computing.

</details>


### [158] [CW-Ghost: Search-Free Granularity Selection for Helper-Thread Prefetching via Capacity Windows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25363)
*Ya Zhang,Tong Lei,Yao Chen,Yonggang Che,Chuanfu Xu,Haozhong Qiu,Yusong Tan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: CW-Ghost 通过单次离线 profiling 估算每迭代的平均缓存行填充量，并结合缓存容量预算推导出 Capacity Window，以此确定 helper-thread 预取的迭代粒度，并结合块级同步限制超前程度。在 Intel 和 AMD 平台上分别实现 1.54x 和 1.33x 的加速，相比 Ghost Threading 提升 15.8% 和 10.8% 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Helper-thread 预取的效果依赖于 helper thread 覆盖的未来迭代范围。固定覆盖范围无法适应不同负载和处理器，而穷举评估候选配置则代价高昂。

Method: 提出 CW-Ghost，通过单次离线 profiling 估算目标区域每迭代的平均需求缓存行填充量，结合缓存容量预算得到 Capacity Window 来确定预取块的迭代粒度，并采用有界块级同步限制 helper thread 超前主线程的块数。

Result: 在 14 个负载实例上，CW-Ghost 在 Intel 和 AMD 平台分别获得 1.54x 和 1.33x 的几何平均加速比；相比 Ghost Threading 性能提升 15.8% 和 10.8%，且达到候选集中经验最优性能的 99% 以上。

Conclusion: 缓存容量约束可以有效指导 helper-thread 预取的粒度选择。

Abstract: Helper-thread prefetching hides the latency of irregular memory accesses by executing address dependency chains ahead of the main thread. However, its effectiveness depends on the range of future iterations covered by the helper thread. A fixed coverage range cannot consistently accommodate different workloads and processors, whereas exhaustively evaluating candidate configurations incurs substantial configuration cost. This paper presents CW-Ghost, which uses a single offline profiling run to estimate the average demand cache line fill volume generated per target iteration in a target region. CW-Ghost combines this estimate with a cache capacity budget to derive a Capacity Window, which determines the iteration granularity of each prefetch chunk. In addition, bounded chunk-level synchronization limits the number of chunks by which the helper thread may run ahead of the main thread. Across 14 workload instances evaluated on Intel and AMD CPU platforms, CW-Ghost achieves geometric mean speedups of 1.54x and 1.33x, respectively, over the original programs. Compared with Ghost Threading, it improves geometric mean performance by 15.8% and 10.8%, respectively, while achieving more than 99% of the empirically optimal performance within the candidate set on both platforms. These results demonstrate that cache capacity constraints can effectively guide the selection of granularity for helper-thread prefetching.

</details>


### [159] [WASP: A Configurable Framework for Portable Stateful Serverless Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25493)
*Matteo Cenzato,Dario d'Abate,Arianna Dragoni,Giacomo Orsenigo,Luca Tosetti,Alessandro Margara*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: WASP是一个可配置的框架，通过解耦WASM运行时、数据存储和生命周期策略，支持边缘-云连续体中的有状态无服务器函数执行，且开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WASM无服务器平台紧耦合于特定执行引擎且主要面向无状态工作负载，无法适应边缘部署的异构性（需要支持有状态应用及不同硬件/工作负载约束）。

Method: 采用严格解耦的可插拔组件架构，允许管理员在不修改应用代码的情况下替换WASM运行时和数据存储，并可配置生命周期和缓存策略以适配不同非功能需求。

Result: 实验表明WASP引入的运行时开销可忽略不计，通过切换运行时、数据存储和策略可展现出截然不同的内存和延迟特性。

Conclusion: WASP能够灵活适应边缘-云连续体的异构约束，验证了其作为有状态无服务器框架的有效性与可配置性。

Abstract: WebAssembly (WASM) is emerging as a lightweight alternative to containers for Function-as-a-Service (FaaS) across the edge-cloud continuum. However, existing WASM-based serverless platforms are tightly coupled to specific execution engines and predominantly designed for stateless workloads. This clashes with the heterogeneity of edge deployments, which demand support for stateful applications under diverse hardware and workload constraints. We introduce WASP, a configurable framework that brings stateful serverless execution to the edge-cloud continuum. By abandoning monolithic architectures in favor of strictly decoupled, pluggable components, WASP lets system administrators swap the WASM runtime and the datastore to fit available resources and application requirements, without altering application code. Configurable lifecycle and caching policies further enable fine-tuning for diverse non-functional requirements. Our experimental evaluation demonstrates that WASP introduces negligible runtime overhead and, by swapping runtimes, datastores, and policies, exposes radically different memory and latency profiles, confirming its adaptability to the heterogeneous constraints of the edge-cloud continuum.

</details>


### [160] [PowerScale: Energy-Efficient Geo-Distributed Model Training with Federated Datacenter Power](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25650)
*Talha Mehboob,Zhe Xu,Michael Zink,David Irwin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: PowerScale是一个分层聚合系统，通过利用广域网延迟层次结构，将站点组织成区域集群并采用同步-异步混合同步模式，在匹配或略微提升训练速度的同时，最高可降低3.9倍能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模AI训练采用单层聚合方式，同步屏障导致快站点空闲、全WAN更新通信能耗高、固定同步频率在训练后期仍支付相同通信成本，能效极低。

Method: 提出PowerScale分层聚合系统：按网络邻近性和电力可用性形成区域集群；集群内高频同步，集群间异步推送预聚合更新；采用自适应同步策略，根据训练进度动态调整同步频率。

Result: 在100站点规模的Flower仿真环境中，PowerScale在匹配或略微提升训练收敛速度的同时，能耗相比单层基线降低高达3.9倍。

Conclusion: 分层聚合与同步-异步混合模式可有效解决地理分布式AI训练中的能耗问题，为大规模跨站点训练提供高能效方案。

Abstract: The power demands of large-scale AI training increasingly exceed the capacity of any single data center, making geo-distributed training across power-constrained sites a practical necessity. Prior work optimizes such training mainly for time-to-accuracy using single-tier aggregation, where every site exchanges model updates directly with a central aggregator over the WAN each synchronization round, without accounting for the energy required to reach convergence. Single-tier aggregation is fundamentally energy-inefficient because synchronization barriers force faster sites to idle, full WAN updates dominate communication energy at scale, and fixed synchronization frequency keeps paying the same communication cost even when updates shrink late in training.
  To address these inefficiencies, we present PowerScale, a hierarchical aggregation system that exploits the latency hierarchy of wide-area networks. PowerScale organizes sites into regional clusters and applies a Sync-Async synchronization modality: sites synchronize frequently with a nearby cluster aggregator over fast local links, while cluster aggregators push pre-aggregated updates asynchronously to a global aggregator over the WAN. PowerScale forms clusters based on both network proximity and power availability, and uses an adaptive synchronization policy that reduces communication energy by adjusting how often clusters synchronize to training progress. This structure shortens synchronization barriers and replaces per-site WAN transmissions with fewer, pre-aggregated transmissions at a lower frequency. We evaluate PowerScale at 100-site scale in a Flower-based simulation environment. PowerScale matches or slightly improves time-to-accuracy compared with single-tier baselines while reducing energy consumption by up to 3.9x.

</details>


### [161] [Hermes: Low Tail-Latency Via Prefix Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25916)
*Alejandro Ranchal-Pedrosa,Dakai Kang,Neil Giridharan,Dahlia Malkhi,Mohammad Sadoghi,Ben Marsh*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Hermes是一种基于前缀共识的两轮旋转领导者BFT协议，通过让过期视图也能完成最终确认，解决了传统领导者协议中因领导者崩溃或缓慢导致的延迟问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于领导者的BFT协议中，领导者崩溃或缓慢会导致视图无法最终确认，超时设置难以平衡：保守超时导致长时间停滞，激进超时浪费视图，这种权衡主导了尾延迟。

Method: 采用n=5f+1进程和前缀共识，投票携带按前缀关系排序的值，仲裁要求可比性而非相等性。每个进程在视图开始时广播合理化提案并投一票，超时时投后备提案，无否决票。结合Autobahn多通道扩散和索引擦除编码提升效率。

Result: 诚实的领导者可在2δ内完成提案最终确认；否则任意n-f票（无需匹配）可确认最重的共同前缀。复杂度为O(n²m̂+λn³)比特和O(n²)消息每视图。

Conclusion: Hermes有效消除了领导者失败导致的视图浪费，显著改善了BFT协议在故障场景下的尾延迟表现。

Abstract: Leader-based BFT protocols finalize through their leaders: a view whose leader is crashed or slow finalizes nothing, and the timeout that ends it admits no good setting. A conservative timeout turns every crashed leader into a long stall; an aggressive one voids the views of leaders that are merely slow. Either way the expired view is wasted, and this trade-off, not the good case, governs tail latency.
  Hermes makes expired views finalize. Hermes is a two-round rotating-leader protocol for $n=5f+1$ processes under partial synchrony built on prefix consensus: votes carry values ordered by a prefix relation, and quorums require comparability rather than equality. Every process broadcasts a justified proposal at view start and casts a single vote, for the leader's proposal upon delivery or for a fallback proposal at the timeout; there are no nullify votes. A timely honest leader finalizes its full proposal in $2δ$. Otherwise, any $n-f$ votes, which need not match, finalize the heaviest common prefix. We instantiate Hermes as a finality gadget over an available chain and over Autobahn-style multi-lane dissemination, where parent-relative delta tipcuts with explicit skips keep independent proposals comparable and sender-indexed erasure-coded amplification keeps complexity at $O(n^2\hat{m}+λn^3)$ bits and $O(n^2)$ messages per view, where $\hat{m}$ is the number of concurrent dissemination lanes in Autobahn and $λ$ the security parameter.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

### [162] [AVE-Compass: Towards Holistic Evaluation for Audio-Video Editing Abilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24821)
*Yuqing Wen,Yukai Huang,Qianqian Xie,Jiangtao Wu,Yibin Lin,Yikai Gu,Jialu Chen,Yuanxing Zhang,Jiaheng Liu*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 提出AVE-Compass基准和AVE-Agent框架，用于评估和提升音视频联合编辑中的跨模态一致性，揭示现有模型在跨模态指令执行方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要评估静默视频的视觉变换或孤立音频编辑，缺乏对复杂音视频联合编辑及跨模态一致性的系统评估。

Method: 构建AVE-Compass基准（145个视频、196条编辑指令、2688个细粒度检查项），通过基于MLLM的清单评判和专用真实性量规进行评估；提出AVE-Agent模块化框架，将复杂指令分解为依赖子任务，并通过自我反思和评估反馈迭代优化。

Result: 最先进模型在执行跨模态指令和保持非目标内容方面仍有困难；AVE-Agent在指令执行、保真度和音视频对齐上表现更优，同时保持竞争性感知质量。

Conclusion: 音视频联合编辑的跨模态一致性仍具挑战，AVE-Agent为这一任务提供了有效的模块化解决方案。

Abstract: While instruction-based video editing has advanced rapidly, real-world videos contain tightly coupled audio and visual signals, and editing one modality often requires coordinated changes in the other. Existing benchmarks primarily evaluate visual transformations on silent clips or isolated audio editing, leaving complex audio-visual editing and cross-modal consistency underexplored. We introduce AVE-Compass, a comprehensive benchmark with 145 curated source videos, 196 audio-visually coupled editing instructions, and 2,688 fine-grained checklist items. It evaluates Instruction Following, Fidelity Preserving, Realism, and Editing Intent through checklist-based MLLM judging and a dedicated realism rubric, complemented by automated cross-modal, video, and audio metrics. Extensive evaluation shows that state-of-the-art models still struggle to execute cross-modal instructions while preserving non-target content. We further propose AVE-Agent, a modular agent framework that decomposes complex instructions into dependent subtasks and iteratively improves editing results through self-reflection and evaluator feedback. AVE-Agent improves instruction execution, Fidelity Preserving, and audio-visual alignment in joint editing while maintaining competitive perceptual quality.

</details>
